# Fabien Roadmap (working checklist)

Master copy: THIS FILE (docs/ROADMAP.md in the repo) from 5 Nov 2026. The "Fabien Roadmap" Claude Doc (https://claude.ai/artifact/7USEXw7SksNFYof24xF2Ca) is a snapshot up to 1 Nov, kept for reading.
Paths like /home/claude/incoming/... were the old cloud workspace and are gone; every original is in Baiyee's Fabien Blueprint folder (same names and times).

Rules for Claude:
- Log every file received in "Files received" (bottom) the same turn it arrives: date, time, what it is, which item. Open every picture once before logging it. (Added 31 Oct after the FFT screenshot in the zip was missed.)
- Read this list (and the doc, for any edits) before every batch, and check every item against it before saying a batch is done.
- One phase per batch: build, test each item, tick it here and in the doc, publish, then stop for Baiyee to play it.
- Every new request gets a number in the right phase. Nothing is dropped quietly.
- "Waiting" items need an answer or art from Suleiman first. Unanswered proposals get built as proposed.
- The best feasible option wins (Baiyee 29 Oct 21:25): when a choice is open, pick what makes the battles feel most premium and is realistic to build, and say what was picked.
- Status: Phases 1, 2, 3 DONE; S.1, S.2 DONE; status and Verse art DONE; Phase 8 DONE (8.8 became 11.5); A.1 music DONE; Phase 9 DONE; battle-screen batch DONE 1 Nov; Phase 11 the Hidden Cavern DONE 1 Nov; clean-up batch DONE 1 Nov; Phase 4 enemy skills DONE 1 Nov, 3 v 3 with Tayo in for the bull (with 11.7 calmer lights). Inbox 5 Nov read and logged (no build yet): new items 1.13 to 1.17, 6.26 to 6.34, Phase 13. Phase 6 leftovers 6.1 to 6.4 DONE 5 Nov. Next: Phase 12 or Phase 10, or the new 6.26 to 6.34 art. H.1 private link waits for Baiyee's first Cloudflare deploy and Access.
- Test harness (5 Nov): dev/tests and dev/browser find the repo from their own location (no /home/claude/fabien paths, no scratch copy). Full suite run 3 times from the repo on 5 Nov: failures vary between runs (mostly sprites not loading inside the 8 second wait, plus null-sprite crashes in n29, n37). Checked against the old code: n37 and n33 fail the same way without the change (pre-existing). n29, three and gaha could not run on the old code here (old paths), so their failures are unverified. Rerun on a quiet machine before trusting the suite. 12 Nov (18.5): done, the suite is steady over 3 runs and takes about 3 minutes; see 18.5.
- Branch wip/oct28-ui-items (commit fccc72d), not on main, not deployed: unreviewed 28 Oct work. Rabdul 20% bigger (reuse for 1.3); battle screen in the OLDER ChatGPT mock style (superseded by Phase 2); items as enemy drops Potion/Ether/Antidote (superseded by Phase 5, Items menu code may be reusable). Check before Phases 1, 2 and 5.

- [x] 1.6 Wall slam, every push. Bug: displacementPath in engagement.js uses (dr, dc)/(-dr, -dc) as the side step, which for diagonal pushes is straight back toward the attacker. New rule: a push blocked by a wall, higher ground or a unit = 2 impact damage + Disoriented until the end of its next turn (confirmed: attack and defense -2, evade -10, magic hits +3 damage).

- [ ] 1.38 Dynamic lighting on Suleiman's sprites (Baiyee 9 Nov). TESTED, NOT ROLLED OUT, decision: HOLD. Built as a test only, off unless `?light` is in the address (src/lighting.js, dev/tools/make_normals.py, dev/browser/lighting.cjs; normal maps for Gaha's 4 standing and battle pictures in public/art/normals/, floor-tile bumps worked out in code). His pictures are untouched. Works: Phaser Light2D, warm flickering light per torch, brazier and lamp (16), orange light on a unit with Fury or Enchant, blue-white flash on each lightning bolt, mood is richer and the Gaha bump lighting shows in the close-up. Problems: (1) close-ups blow out to orange (floor and Gaha wash out, enemies lose their blue); needs lower fire intensity and a cooler fill; (2) Light2D has no flip support, so mirrored enemies get left/right-swapped bumps (not obvious yet); (3) frame rate: the test browser has no real GPU (software GL), plain 13 fps vs lit 2 to 3 fps, so a real phone number is still missing; the cost is real (one pass over every light for every lit pixel, and lit objects stop batching). To decide: run `?light` on Baiyee's phone and read the frame rate, then tune. If it holds 30 fps on the phone, roll-out needs: a normal map per picture (about 490 pictures; the Python tool does a batch run), one mirrored map set for flipped enemies or a custom shader, floor and wall tiles and props bumped, and only the 6 nearest lights switched on at a time. TONED DOWN 9 Nov (Baiyee likes the ?light look, keep it behind ?light): torch light 1.7 to 1.0 and radius 260 to 210, aura 1.7 to 1.2, fill changed from warm 0xc8b49a to cool 0x9db4d0, ambient cooler; rollout decision waits for the new Blender fire and water (1.39).

Original text: Smoke Bomb cloud looks bad (Baiyee, 8 Nov, after 13.26): a flat, opaque white blob with a hard diamond edge at the bottom (left over from the floor tile), too bright for the cave, and static. Fix: (1) remove the diamond edge and feather the bottom into a soft fade; (2) warm grey tint, 60 to 70% opacity, so the tile and units show through; (3) alive: the 6 growing frames play when thrown, then the full cloud drifts and breathes gently, with a few soft smoke puffs (fxEmitter, the 'fx-dot' scaled up, grey, low alpha) rolling off it; (4) when it clears, the dissipating frames play while fading. If his pictures still look pasted on, use a particle-only cloud instead.

- [x] 1.45 Archer bug: she always faces one way when she aims or shoots (Baiyee 14 Nov). Cause found on a contact sheet: on Suleiman's full-quality sheets (M2 recut) the two views from behind run up-left then up-right, as the game expects, but the front views do not run down-left then down-right. Battle stance and shot: BOTH front figures point the bow right. Aim (bow primed): the third points right, the fourth left. So with a hero down-left of her (where heroes usually stand) she aimed and shot to the right. The code picked the right picture names all along; the names were wrong. Built 14 Nov: dev/tools/fix_archer_facings.py changes art.json (down-left battle and shot are the third figure mirrored, the aim swaps its two front figures, the Poison Arrow shot uses her full-quality back views when she shoots away from us). Also: a plain shot never showed the aim picture, so she now draws and aims for a fifth of a second, then lets fly; and a target straight above or below her on the screen no longer mixes a left stance with a right shot. Checked: every four-view set of hers on contact sheets (the Poison Arrow crouch, dagger, dodge and flaming-bow sets were already right). Test n60 (the aim and shot pictures are measured: which side the bow reaches to, from all four sides, mirrored ones too). GIF: Fabien Blueprint/ARCHER FIXES 2026-11-14/archer_faces.gif.
- [x] 1.46 Archer bug: she never switches to her dagger up close (Baiyee 14 Nov). Cause: the weapon switch (13.29) was only made by her AI after it had walked, and her AI always walked away first and shot from range, so the dagger came out only when she was boxed in. A Poison Arrow she was aiming could also fly at a hero who had walked right up to her (point-blank). Built 14 Nov, the rule now: (1) between turns she holds the dagger while a foe she can see stands next to her and the bow otherwise; it is a reflex, free, and does not use her one switch (a hero who walks up to her sees her draw it at once; undoing the move puts it away); (2) her AI, with a foe next to her, stabs it with the dagger first, then steps back with the steps she has left (13.53); at range she shoots as before; Smoke Bomb when hurt or cornered still comes first; (3) an aim at a foe that has come next to her is dropped at the start of her turn ("Too close"), and she keeps her whole turn. This replaces the 13.29 line "the AI takes the dagger after she has moved". Test n60. GIF: archer_dagger.gif.
- [x] 1.47 Invisible does not work (Baiyee 14 Nov). Cause: tapping a foe to walk up and strike it (the usual way to attack) never asked whether the foe can be seen; only the Attack and Skills lists did. And a hidden enemy was drawn at 60 percent with a bright gold glow, the look made for the player's own hidden Layra. Built 14 Nov. THE RULE (Smoke Bomb and Husk alike): a hidden unit cannot be picked as a target by the other side: no attack, no walk-up attack, no skill, no clash, and an arrow aimed before it hid does not fly ("Lost sight"). Nobody turns to face it. It still stands on its tile (it blocks the way). The exception is area effects: Lightning's jump and Tayo's Sonic Burst hit a hidden unit caught in them, and a hit that does damage shows it again ("Revealed"; with Husk the decoy goes too). The enemy AI decides only on what it can see (it will not clap because of a hidden hero, but the clap hits her if she is in the ring). The look: the player's own hidden hero keeps the gold glow at 60 percent; a hidden enemy is a faint grey shape at 30 percent. Checked for the enemy AI: attack, Poison Arrow, clash, Element Blast and a whole turn standing next to a hidden Layra. Test n60; dev/browser/hidden_look.cjs photographs it. GIF: archer_invisible.gif.

CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED 15 Nov: Infuse in Water was not a spirit's blessing at all. castSpell sent a Water reading to castHeal (the Sage's herb-pouch heal), which raises the herb pouch picture and throws a pouch at the ally for an instant +8; the other three elements gave a Verse (Stone, Swiftness, Fury). Infuse's text and the unit card show RENEW. Test n62 (the old Water check, +8 at once, now checks Renewal: no pouch, no instant heal, the tick, 3 turns).

Baiyee: which screen showed the line? Nothing was removed. 19 Nov, second look: the exact text "+0 MP" is in no build since 15 Nov (searched every MP text the game writes, and a test casts Infuse in all four readings on allies with and without MP and reads every word on screen). Infuse is not meant to restore MP. - [x] 1.68 Knock-back: the figure vanishes and appears where it was sent (Suleiman 20 Nov 07:27: "new bug, when a character's supposed to get knocked back, they disappear and appear in the location they've been sent flying to"). Probably the 13.54 or 19 Nov push fixes (a figure left off its tile) or the 13.53 walking changes skipped the slide. Find the cause with dev/browser/knock.cjs before fixing. Found 20 Nov (a real browser, every knock-back in a played battle traced frame by frame, dev/browser/knock_trace.cjs): a push moves the unit on the board at once, and since 13.54 the clash lunge and hit recoil draw the body from the tile it stands on NOW, so the body jumped to the landing tile during the strike, and the knockback then flew it in from the old tile (a 55 px jump in one frame, then the flight back). Built: bodyHome(unit): until its knockback starts (knockPending) the body is drawn from the tile it was pushed from (pushedFrom, also for Gadara's pulls); a push never drawn is cleared at the next turn. Battle trace after: no jump over 13 px. Test n88.

Built 20 Nov: a new pose 'fistRaised' in art.json e1 (the same four pictures as his victory, tayo_n6b_victory_f1 to f4: one fist high above his head, the only raised-fist pictures on his sheets; same files so the same ruler scales, no new size to pin), used as the wind-up, then groundPound as the fist hits the floor; the wind-up is a beat longer (330 ms, was 220) so the raised fist is seen. Element Blast keeps blastCharge. Tests n41 and n76 now require the raised fist and forbid blastCharge and blastWind in Earth Drive; filmed with gifs.cjs tayo_earth_drive (the arm up in frames 5 to 7). If Suleiman means a different picture for the raised fist, change the files in poses.fistRaised.
- [x] 1.74 Tooltips vanished before they could be read (Baiyee 20 Nov, desktop). Built 20 Nov: a hovered skill or button shows its box after about 300 ms (was 500) and keeps it for as long as the mouse stays on that row (the 3 second limit is gone). It closes when the mouse leaves, when a choice is made (any press) or on Esc. A long press on a phone (450 ms) shows it and it stays until the next tap (a press on any row puts it away). src/tooltip.js (TIP_HOVER_MS, no hide timer), src/hud.js; hud.cjs checks it (still open after 4 seconds, Esc, 150 ms no / 600 ms yes, gone on leaving); n72 updated.
- [x] 1.75 The gold pointer stuck on the first row of the skills list and every HUD menu (Baiyee 20 Nov, desktop: moving the mouse over a row should highlight that row, also after scrolling). CAUSE: domHud drawMenu threw away the remembered row on the very next frame (the cursor had no key yet, so it never matched the list's key), so the highlight always went back to the first usable row. Built 20 Nov: paintCursor reads the mouse's real place every frame (elementFromPoint), so the pointer follows the mouse over every row, also after a redraw, a page change or a list change under a still mouse; touch and keyboard keep the row the game itself lit (the first usable row of a list when the mouse is elsewhere). hud.cjs checks it row by row. Also: hud.cjs found its clash-meter skull by file name in src, which is a blob address since 18.48; it now takes the last picture of the meter.
- [x] 1.65 The Bull Commander heals during a clash (Suleiman 17 Nov 21:16 and voice 1657: "why this mofo heal while we were clashing... he can't be healing during clash"). Check which rule lets an item or heal run inside a clash and block it for every unit, enemy and hero. Built 19 Nov. CAUSE: the enemy AI checked only the giver: an enemy next to a badly hurt ally handed him a healing item even while that ally was locked in a clash (reproduced: Tayo gave the locked bull his Shea balm, 14 to 24 health). FIX, for every fighter, hero and enemy: nobody locked in a clash uses an item or is handed one (items.js enemyItemChoice, enemyAllyItemChoice; itemTargets in BattleScene.js), Herbs and an Infuse that would give Renewal cannot target a locked fighter (healsTarget), and a Renewal already on him does not heal while he is locked (it still counts down). Test n79.
- [x] 1.76 Replaying a match: the HUD does not come back (Suleiman 21 Nov 09:20: "game's ui doesn't come up when you try to replay a match, have to refresh in order to get it back up"). CAUSE: the result panel sets `resultShown`, which hides the HTML HUD (domHud.sync), and `create()` never put it back to false; Play again restarts the same scene object, so the flag stayed true and the overlay stayed `visibility:hidden` (the canvas parts it replaces are not drawn, so the screen had no HUD at all until a page refresh). Built 21 Nov: `create()` resets it. Test: dev/browser/replay.cjs (a real tap on Play again, then checks the new battle's units and that the overlay is visible with its menu; fails on the old build, passes on both phone and desktop).
- [x] 1.77 Clash damage bonus: check that hitting a fighter caught in a clash gets its bonus (Suleiman 21 Nov 08:59: "not sure the damage buff from attacking an enemy caught in an engagement is active, cause even the archer does abysmal damage"). Baiyee: show the numbers. CHECKED 21 Nov: it was NOT active, because it never existed. The only thing a fighter caught in a clash gave its attackers was +10 to hit (distractedHitBonus); the damage was the plain attack minus defense. Numbers before (damage free = in a clash, hit chance free to in a clash): Archer on Sudeen 1 = 1, 80 to 90 percent; on Gaha 3 = 3; on Layra 4 = 4; Rabdul on Sudeen 3 = 3, Gaha 5 = 5; Bull on Gaha 6 = 6; Gaha on the Archer 7 = 7; Layra on Rabdul 5 = 5. So the archer (her attack is 5 in units.json) really does 1 to 4. PICKED: a flat +2 damage on every hit on a fighter locked in a clash with someone else (engagement.json distractedDamageBonus; same place as the flank bonus a Stunned unit gives, +3; flat so it helps a weak hitter like the archer most; it applies to every attacker and every skill that uses getDamage, never to the two inside the clash against each other, whose beat blows are their own). After: Archer on Sudeen 3, on Gaha 5, on Layra 6; a mage 5 to 7. Change the number in engagement.json if it feels wrong. Test: dev/tests/n97.cjs (needs no picture server; fails on the old code). Balance check with the full suite and bot battles at the end of the 21 Nov batch.
- [x] 1.78 Kofi's AI never uses Infuse or Haunt, even on low health (Suleiman 21 Nov 01:05: "playing against kofi and he's on low health and not making use of his infusion abilities, like at all. not even casting haunt"). CAUSE: the enemy turn (enemyAttack) had a branch for each enemy's special moves (Enchant, Element Blast, Clap, Shield Toss, Lunge, Poison Arrow) and none for Kofi's two, so with Haunt and Infuse never chosen his only action was the free Spirit Bolt, on any health. Built 21 Nov: BattleScene.shamanChoice(unit), called before the plain attack. INFUSE (reach 3, himself too, never on someone who already wears a Verse): Water/Renewal to a friend at 70 percent health or less (the most hurt first, himself on a tie); Earth/Stone to a hurt friend in the fight, else the friend nearest a foe; Swiftness to a friend at 40 percent or less, else the hardest hitter in the fight; Fire/Fury to the hardest hitter in the fight. HAUNT (reach 4): the most dangerous foe in reach that is not Disoriented yet, unless the bolt would finish it; he keeps 2 MP back for Infuse while a friend is near. Test: dev/tests/n98.cjs (`FABIEN_MAP=cavern FABIEN_BENCH= FABIEN_SIDES='{"h4":"enemy"}'`, 11 checks incl. a real enemy turn). Two bot battles with him on the enemy side: Haunt x11, Infuse x7, no errors. Not done: him retreating when low (13.69 personalities).
- [x] 1.79 Renewal should heal as soon as it is cast, then every turn it lasts (Suleiman 21 Nov 02:00: "renewal should heal immediately upon casting and for whatever turns it lasts"). CAUSE: Renewal is a Verse whose heal runs in tickRenewal at the start of the blessed unit's turns, and casting it only set the Verse (1.49). Built 21 Nov: castSpell calls renewalTick(target, ..., cast) the moment Renewal is given, so the first heal (about 5 percent, at least 3 points) and one weak status lifted come at once with a "+3 Renewal" number, then the same at the start of each of its turns while it lasts (3 turns); the amounts are unchanged, texts in spells.json updated. Not changed: a fighter locked in a clash still gets no heal from the ticks (1.65). Test: n62 (the old check "heals nothing at once" now reads "heals as soon as it is cast").
- [x] 1.80 Singularity should deal damage when it lands (Suleiman 21 Nov 02:03: "Singularity should do damage. Pay off needs to be worth the wait"). Baiyee: suggest a number and say what was picked. CAUSE: Singularity only pulled; its only damage was 2 for a foe stopped by a wall or another unit (and fall damage). SUGGESTED AND PICKED: every foe still standing when the pull ends takes 1.5 times a normal blow from Gadara, a sure hit (the pull was no attack roll), on top of any slam or fall. His normal blow does 5 to 8 (Sudeen 5, Gaha and Alagara 7, Layra and Kofi 8), so Singularity does about 8 to 12 to each foe, and more to one dragged next to him, who is Off-Balance (defense -2): 11 and 15 in the test. Three foes caught is 30 to 40 damage, a whole hero's health, for a full turn of charging, the Exposed turn after, once a battle, and it can be broken. Why 1.5: the same share as his Heavy Cleave, and it follows his attack and the foe's defense like every other blow. Change `damageShare` in kings.json (1.5) to tune it. Built in kingsScene.resolveSingularity; texts updated. Test: n86 (two new checks).
- [x] 1.81 Mantle needs a longer cast range (Suleiman 21 Nov 02:13: "mantle should have a wider cast range"). CAUSE: Mantle's range was 1, "every ally next to him". PICKED 3 tiles (the same as Singularity's area and Kofi's Infuse), so an ally one step behind the line is covered too, and he no longer has to stand among them. Built 21 Nov: spells.json mantle.range 3, kingsScene.mantleAllies (the cast and the AI use the same list, so the AI casts when an ally within 3 has no Verse), picking Mantle lights the 25 tiles it reaches (like Singularity), texts updated. Also guarded a latent error when cast with no Verse. Test: n87 (3 away gets it, 4 away does not, the lit tiles).
- [x] 1.84 Mantle gives no special skills (Suleiman 21 Nov: Sonic Javelin, Blitz and the others a Verse turns a move into). CAUSE: Mantle gives Alagara's Verse at half strength (power 0.5) and applyBuff scaled the MP the Verse lends by the same half, so a fighter with no MP of his own got 1.5 MP while every Verse move (Sonic Javelin, Blitz, Fury Dive, Fury Charge, Shield Ram) costs 3; activeMove saw too little MP and gave back the plain move, so the skill never showed. Built 21 Nov: a half-strength Verse still lends enough MP for one Verse move (status.js applyBuff); its numbers (attack +1, evade and so on) and its one turn stay at half strength. Test: n87 (Fury: Fury Dive and Fury Charge, one move then the plain one is back; Swiftness: Sonic Javelin and Blitz; the Verse stays power 0.5 for one turn).
- [x] 1.82 (built 21 Nov with 19.6, Batch 7) The archer can poison her weapon at any time, and a new move that raises her attack's damage (Suleiman 21 Nov 02:18 to 02:19: "the archer should be able to apply poison to her weapon at any point in time. She needs a move that increases the damage of her attack, call it Prime Bow, it takes a turn to charge, the trade-off is she gets a longer range of attack"). Needs a design (what poisoning costs, how Prime Bow ends). Built 21 Nov (numbers picked): Poison Weapon, free at any time in her turn (before or after moving or acting; not inside a clash, where her menu is the clash menu): her next hit that lands (arrow, dagger or Pinning Shot) poisons like Poison Arrow (3 a turn for 3 turns, never the last point); ready again after 3 turns; the coat stays until a hit lands. Prime Bow takes her action (and her steps) and ends her turn; at the start of her next turn the bow is primed: her Attack with the bow has +4 attack and reaches 2 to 7 (2 more); one shot spends it, hit or miss; unused by the end of that turn, or caught in a clash, it is lost. Spent only by her Attack (not Poison Arrow or Pinning Shot). Ready again after 3 turns (spells.json primeBow, poisonWeapon). After acting, her Skills button stays live while Poison Weapon is ready and the list shows only the free skills. Her AI coats at once when a foe is near, and draws when nobody is in reach or her plain shot would do 2 or less with nobody within 3 tiles. Test n106.

- [x] 1.116 Compact phone menu (Baiyee 27 Nov): rows about 44 px apart, the whole menu no taller than about 25 percent of the screen, each row's tap area fills its row (no extra padding bands), beside the active fighter and never over a fighter. Now on a phone the band fills the whole row and rows are 44 real px apart when the menu then fits in a quarter of the screen; else they close up to 40 (390 x 844: 40, 25 percent; 412 x 915: 44, 25 percent). His two numbers cannot both hold on a short phone (5 rows 44 apart are 220 px, a quarter of 667 is 167), so on a 375 x 667 the main commands stand in two columns 44 apart (Attack | Engage, Skills | Items, Wait): 21 percent (picked by Claude: the only layout that keeps both). Never over a fighter: the menu used to avoid only the fighter whose turn it is; now every fighter's body counts against a spot (more than any number of blue tiles), and it tries a ladder of heights so on a zoomed-in phone it steps past a neighbour's head. Measured in 40 s of real play (m7.cjs): over a fighter in 1 to 5 percent of moments (the moment a fighter steps), was 16 to 24. m7.cjs also stops counting moments when the camera is still gliding to the fighter (he is off the screen). - [x] 1.117 Tooltips on a phone only on a long press; a tap on Skills opens the list at once; a single tap on a skill picks it and lights its targets at once, holding only shows its info (Baiyee and Suleiman 27 Nov: "you have to hold the skill, then tap a fighter"). Built 27 Nov. CAUSE: a row acted the moment the finger landed, and the HTML menu was redrawn under the finger, so the lift never reached the row. Two things followed: the half-second tooltip timer was never stopped (a tooltip after every quick tap), and the phone browser's pretend mouse press and click after a touch (iPhone Safari sends them unless the touch is cancelled, and a redrawn row cannot cancel it) landed on the board where the menu had been, read as "a tap on empty ground: cancel", so the skill just picked was put away at once. A long press sends no pretend click, which is why holding worked. Fixed: a finger acts when it lifts (MenuRow.release; the lift is followed on the window), a hold shows the info only, a finger sliding off a row is not a tap, a mouse still acts on the click; any mouse press, click or hover within 800 ms of a finger is stopped (domHud). Test n121; turn_clicks.cjs now taps as a phone does (down, 110 ms, up, then the pretend mouse click) and checks a hold and one skill of each kind by taps: on a foe (Gaha's Spear Throw), on an ally (Layra's Swiftness), on a tile (Husk), on self (Sudeen's Herbs), phone and desktop ALL PASSED.

CAUSE: the floor clean-up (detile, deslab in make_newclass.py) goes by colour, and Leol's tan paws and fur and the Thief's cream trousers and sandals are the colour of the terracotta tile, so they went with it. Fixed at the cutter (add_body): a person-segmentation model (rembg u2net_human_seg, already on this Mac) tells body from floor by shape; what it calls body comes back from the untouched cut, and everything the colour clean-up kept stays (the model drops a dagger or a sling cord). The model is asked several ways and the answer that keeps least of the tile's side faces wins (feet stand on the top, never on the sides); if all keep the tile (the Thief's grassy tile with its plate), it is shown only the kept figure with an 8 px band of the untouched picture round it, and joins the broken legs up; restored patches in the paving's own colours are dropped. One picture judged worse by eye keeps its old cut (thief_victory_2: sandy floor came back). Result: paws back in Leol's standing, battle, weak, hurt, guard and hammer poses; the Thief's legs and sandals back in battle, hurt, parry, steal, victory, weak. Still patchy: the Thief's standing 1, 3, 4, weak 2 and slash (pale tile edges where the shins were) and Leol lying down: a sheet on a plain ground would fix them for good (asked in 1.113). Their art.json heights grew with the taller standing pictures (Leol 92 to 114, the Thief 70 to 79, at the cutter) so they are drawn exactly as big as before; sizes re-pinned (pose_size.py), the Thief's victory over his crouch noted as tall; Leol's weak down pair looked at and marked ok (facing_known.json); normal maps and WebP twins made. - [x] 1.128 URGENT (Baiyee 27 Nov, desktop): the battle froze after Layra died on her own turn (low, Husk, then Swift, a long walk onto the hidden Thief's trap by a lamp, 6 damage, dead mid-walk; nobody got a turn again). CAUSE: a player's walk has nothing to do when it ends (moveUnit is called with no follow-up); the turn ends only when the player taps, and a fallen hero has no menu, so the dead fighter kept the turn for ever. The AI's turns were not stuck (they always reach enemyWrapUp), but a dead AI fighter still went on to enemyAttack. Built 27 Nov: BattleScene.watchDeadTurn, called from defeat() when the fighter falls in its own turn: a player's turn ends by itself 0.6 seconds after the board settles (nothing busy, nobody flying); the safety net ends ANY turn whose fighter has fallen 5 seconds after the fall if no new turn has started; if that side is out the result shows instead. enemyAttack does nothing for a fallen fighter (no blow from beyond) and goes to the wrap-up. Test n123 (one case for each way of dying mid-move: the trap, Rabdul's Spear Ward, Leol's Suppressive Fire, a slam after the trap's throw, an AI fighter on a trap, the safety net with the game stuck busy, the last hero on a trap shows Defeat); with the fix switched off 6 of its checks fail.

Suleiman's golden Aegis GIF is kept only as a fallback. Test n117 (1.127 checks).

## Phase 3: Intercept and Verse-changed moves (all in spells.json) - DONE
Notes: spells.json "verse": {of, with, jobs}; status.js activeMove/verseMovesOf/lent MP/moveRules/rooted/burn; BattleScene castIntercept (effect lock/knockback/strike/stun), javelinForm/javelinLine/castSonicJavelin/playMove; art.json h2 "moves"; fx sj_* and swift_*; terrain.json climbLimit 1; test n32. Bot 12 battles: 3 wins (was 4; the bot doesn't use Verse moves).
- [x] 3.1 Intercept = long-range engage: charge up to 4 tiles, half damage, lock in a clash; takes over an ally's clash and frees the ally. No stun by default. Turn ends by itself when it locks.
- [x] 3.2 Verse-move system: while a Verse lasts the move's name/effect changes; reverts when it fades; preview text.
- [x] 3.3 Sudeen + Stone: Intercept knocks back 2 tiles instead of clashing (wall slam rule 1.6). Built as Shield Ram.
- [x] 3.4 Sudeen + Fury: Intercept as a straight full-damage attack, no clash (full damage = proposal). Built as Fury Charge.
- [x] 3.5 Sudeen + Swift: Intercept stuns (replaces his earlier multi-hit idea). Built as Blitz: half damage, stun, no clash.
- [x] 3.6 Gaha + Swift: Sonic Javelin with his sheets. Hits every unit in its line (confirmed); +2 range over Spear Throw (proposal); pierces, so higher blocks don't stop it. Built: reach 5, never misses, enemies only (allies passed through).
- [x] 3.7 Layra + Stone (on herself): lightning pushes the target 1 tile. Built as Thunderclap (still chains).
- [x] 3.8 Layra + Swift: moves ignore height and gaps (hop between high ground). Built with a new walking rule: step up at most 1 level (terrain.json climbLimit); Swift-Layra ignores it and flies over units; the 2-high pillar is her perch.
- [x] 3.9 Layra + Fury: fire spell with Burn (proposal 2 a turn for 3 turns; Herbs cure it). Built as Wildfire: never the last point, no chain.
- [x] 3.10 Swift looks: swirl while active, trail while moving (zip: NEW EFFECTS).
- [x] 3.11 Sonic Javelin has three forms, one per row of his Sonic Javelin sheet (a23d8ed6-image.png, 29 Oct 19:19): row 1 up close = dash in and bring the spear down in a whirl; rows 2-3 at a distance = the throw with sonic rings, hits everyone in the line; row 4 = follow-up after a knockback: zip next to the knocked-back enemy (like a teleport) and sweep low with an earth burst. Built: close = 2 tiles or less; sweep = foe Off-Balance or Disoriented, 1.5x.
- [x] 3.12 Gaha + Stone: guard while standing still: if he didn't move, half damage and can't be pushed until his next turn (confirmed "solid implementation"). ROOTED tag.
- [x] 3.13 MP bar only when it means something (Baiyee 29 Oct 21:25): magic users (Layra; Rabdul once he has Enchant) always show MP. Non-magic units (Sudeen, Gaha, bull, archer) show it only while a Verse is on them. Built: the Verse lends its MP (3, or 6 when echoed, i.e. mp x power); each Verse-changed move costs 3 of it; when spent the move reverts to its normal form; leftovers go when the Verse fades. A Verse that changes none of the unit's moves lends nothing (no bar).
- [x] 3.14 Gaha + Fury = a dive attack. Art arrived 30 Oct 17:58 (04_fury_motion: Verse Fury motion reference; 06_gaha_fury_moves: 12 frames incl. a leap and a downward slam). Proposal: leap up to 3 tiles at a foe over heads and walls, slam for 1.5x a normal blow, enemies next to the landing knocked Off-Balance; uses the Verse's MP.
- [x] 3.15 Layra's Husk under Swift: the decoy bursts in a howling gust when struck (Baiyee and Suleiman 30 Oct 17:58). Proposal: the attacker and anyone else next to the decoy are blown 2 tiles away (wall slam rule); the gust itself does no damage.
Verse moves round two DONE 31 Oct (39b585b): spells.json furyDive (kind dive, verse of spearThrow with fury, range 3, damageShare 1.5, mp 3); BattleScene castFuryDive (sweepSpot landing, arc tween, Off-Balance to enemies next to landing), castIntercept plays art.json h1 moves furiousCharge/shieldRam (frames 0 ready, 1-2 charge steps, 3+ strike), castHusk sets decoy.gust under Swift, popDecoy -> decoyGust (enemies next to decoy pushed 2, pushLoser from decoy tile); frames cut by dev/tools/make_moves31.py; test dev/tests/n36.cjs; pictures dev/tests/shots-vm2.cjs.

## Phase 12: slopes and half steps (proposal, Baiyee 1 Nov)
In FFT the highlighted tiles bend because many tiles are ramps (a slope rising from one edge to the other) and heights go in half steps (his screenshot: Height 7.5). Our tiles are all flat-topped blocks, so highlights are flat diamonds.
- [ ] 12.1 Ramp tiles: a tile can slope up from one side to the other; its ground picture, sides and highlight lie on the slope; units stand at its middle height.
- [ ] 12.2 Half steps: heights in 0.5s; climbing a ramp counts as half a step; pushes, falls and line of sight use the real slope.
- [ ] 12.3 The cavern gets stairs and ramps where he wants them (his layout, or him marking them).

- [ ] 10.2 Skills: Drawn Blade (sheathed-draw stance in a clash), Claim (take an adjacent unit's buff, ally or enemy, keep 2 beats), Mantle (share a Verse with adjacent allies at half strength).
- [ ] 10.3 Verse skills: Gale Cut (Swift, line slash hitting every enemy on it), Rending Draw (Fury, ignores part of Guard, pushes further), Unmoved (Stone, cancels a push, half-strength Press back).
- [ ] 10.4 Crowned Cut finisher at +3, changing with his Verse (with Phase 7).

### (REVISED 19 Nov: build from 10.23 to 10.29, which replaces the numbers below where they differ.) Gadara the Greater, the Hollow King (class sheet "Gadara_the_Greater_Class_Sheet.pdf", chat file 1451, 13 Nov 17:46; read in full 16 Nov; copy in INBOX/2026-11-15b). Gadara is MALE (Baiyee 16 Nov). NOT BUILT: Phase 10 is not started (Baiyee 16 Nov: write it down, do not build it).
A towering, immovable king who fights as a bruiser. Guardian, Sovereign subset, foil to King Alagura. Draft for playtesting: every number is a placeholder. His weapon is a sword-staff (a narrow double-edged blade at one end, a hooked fork of two curled hooks at the other). He wears a carved ancestral mask at the belt: donning it starts the Hollow Rite and he becomes a caster of one spell, Singularity, a black hole that drags enemies to him. Alagura takes buffs and spreads them; Gadara strips and suppresses buffs and gathers everything onto himself. Unmasked he controls the front line; masked he sets up the whole battlefield for one decisive turn.
- [ ] 10.7 Profile: resource SP plus the Mask Gauge; core stance Press and Anchor (held while channelling); stats Attack high, Defense high, Resolve very high, HP high, Focus high, Agility low (slow: Press and kiting punish him). Two modes: Unmasked (Bruiser: blade end for cuts, fork end for hooks, calm upright pose, mask at the belt) and Masked (the Hollow Rite: fork raised as the casting staff, one hand over the face, weapon overhead; his body stays the same size in both modes).
- [ ] 10.8 Weapon skills: Reverse Grip (free, once a turn: toggles the active end; blade = damage bonus, fork = hooks and buff stripping). Snare Hook (stance skill, 2 SP, fork: a won Beat pulls the target 1 tile toward him, a reverse displacement; beats Slip). Sunder Boon (stance skill, 2 SP, fork: tears one active buff off the target; feeds the Mask Gauge). Twin Prongs (attack, 1 SP, fork: hits the target and the unit behind it in a line). Heavy Cleave (stance skill, 2 SP, blade: a slow heavy Press that beats Guard and breaks armour).
- [ ] 10.9 Dominion (passive): enemy buffs within 2 tiles are 25 percent weaker; he also resists displacement.
- [ ] 10.10 Mask Gauge and Don the Mask (stance skill, 1 SP, needs a full gauge: enters the Hollow Rite). The gauge fills when he strips a buff, holds an engagement for several Beats, or takes damage, so he must play the bruiser first. Casting empties it: Singularity is available about once a battle.
- [ ] 10.11 Singularity (channel spell, spends the gauge; "Umbra" in Suleiman's effect files IS this spell, Baiyee 16 Nov, so the two Umbra GIFs, 6.59, are its effect). A one-Beat telegraphed channel: the target area is shown a round ahead; Counterspell, Hex, a broken channel, or killing or interrupting him during the telegraph round stops it. Pull: every enemy within 3 tiles is drawn up to 3 tiles toward him, stopping at obstacles and other units; allies are unaffected. Buffs: pulled enemies' buffs are suppressed for one Beat. Crowding: units that collide take damage; units that end next to him are Off-Balance. Terrain: pulling an enemy down from high ground applies fall damage; dragging a unit across fire, mud or water applies that tile's effect. Weather: clustered enemies take more from fire, storm and lightning. Aftermath: he is Drained for a round (Poise reduced, Guard -1) and the mask comes off. Counters: high Resolve resists pull distance; a Guardian's Hold the Line holds an ally in place; Slip or Break can escape the area; Anchored units barely move.
- [ ] 10.12 Sovereign's Verdict (finisher at +3 momentum, with Phase 7): Unmasked, he hooks the target and slams it down. Masked: Collapse, where every unit pulled by Singularity takes a burst.
- [ ] 10.13 Foil to Alagura: Claim against Sunder Boon (both want the same buffs: who gets there first); Alagura's Gale Cut line attack is strongest against enemies Gadara has pulled together. Party synergy: area attacks (fire, storm, lightning) on the cluster; the Ranger's Volley and Suppressing Fire on the pulled group; the Enchanter's Stone Verse keeps him anchored through Drained and Renewal keeps him upright; a Guardian or Warden covers him during the telegraph and Drained rounds; a Scout's Reveal shows enemy buffs, so he knows when Sunder Boon is worth the cost. Weaknesses: hooks need adjacent targets, buff stripping only matters when enemies are buffed, Singularity is rare, telegraphed and counterable, and he is exposed while Drained.
- [ ] 10.14 Systems the sheet needs that do not exist yet: SP (a skill resource), the Mask Gauge, an Anchor stance, a reverse displacement (a pull: the push code in BattleScene.pushPlan runs away from the pusher), buff stripping and suppression, a one-Beat telegraphed channel shown a round ahead, Counterspell or Hex that breaks a channel, the Drained status, Resolve as a stat that resists displacement, a Hold the Line skill, Reveal. First version proposal: use what exists (SP like Poise, Anchor as Guard), as for King Alagura (10.5).
- [ ] 10.15 Art and production: the existing turnaround (8 directions, mask at the belt, weapon at the side) is the neutral base; the masked pose sheet becomes the Don the Mask animation; keep the weapon baked into most frames; the black hole is a separate overlay with a telegraph ring anchored to his tile (the Umbra GIFs, 6.59); short animations wanted for Reverse Grip, Snare Hook, Heavy Cleave and Drained. 14 Nov 06:09: more "kingly abilities" sheets are coming from Suleiman (not received).
- [ ] 10.16 Open questions from the sheet, for playtesting: how fast should the Mask Gauge fill, and is once a battle right for Singularity? Should Singularity be able to pull allies as an opt-in for repositioning? Is a 3 tile radius and 3 tile pull strong enough on larger maps? From the Gaha sheet: a pull skill was dropped from Gaha because Gadara owns it, and Gaha's Counterstep and Lunge still need checking against his kit (13.59).

The traces showed it: the buttons were always last drawn in the enemy's turn, and each NO BUTTONS came right after an OFF TILE wait. FIX (dev/tests/bot.cjs): the second looks and NO BUTTONS run only if it is still the same hero's calm turn, and the loop starts again with whoever acts now. Every enemy within 3 tiles is pulled up to 3 tiles in a straight line toward him, stopping at blocked tiles and other units; allies are not affected. A unit stopped by a wall, a higher level or another unit takes 2 damage and is Disoriented. Enemies that end next to his allies trigger Relay Strikes. Aftermath: he is Exposed for 1 turn (defense -2, evade -10), the mask comes off, the gauge resets to 0. Once per battle. Built 20 Nov: cast = mask on, turn spent, the tiles within 3 marked (violet, the Umbra orb over him); resolves at the start of his next turn (nearest first; slam 2 damage and Disoriented; next to him Off-Balance; relays); Exposed (defense -2, evade -10), gauge 0, then he plays his turn. A Stun, fall or slam breaks it (gauge empties, use kept). AI: 3 or more foes within 3, or 2 with one to drag in.

## Phase 16: Effects flair (Baiyee 8 Nov, planned, NOT built)
- [x] 16.1 Effects-flair pass with Phaser particle emitters and the fx art we already have (public/art/fx). Each Verse gets a looping signature look plus a step effect and a hit effect, and engagements get more punch. Lens: Juiciness (more reaction from every action), held in check by Clarity (on a phone the board must stay readable).
  - What exists today (build on it, don't replace it): auras kept by `keepAura` in BattleScene (swiftAura, furyAura, bulwarkAura, slowAura in src/spellArt.js), swiftTrail and speedStreak on Swift steps, clashFlash (two blades meeting) and the impact burst in src/effects.js, the clash camera zoom in `playBeat` (1.18, or 1.35 on the opening or a decisive beat), the 0.35 finishing-blow slow-mo through `setSpeed('slowMo')`, and the 'fx-dot' particle texture (src/effects.js, src/atmosphere.js).
  - Rules for every effect: (1) at most 3 looping emitters on the board at once at full strength (the active unit and the units under a Verse that are on screen); others drop to half the particles. (2) Each emitter has a hard cap (12 to 20 live particles for loops, 30 for a one-off burst). (3) Loops sit behind the figure and stay below 60% opacity (raised to about 80% for particles on 8 Nov, see 16.3), so faces, HP bars and tile highlights are never covered. (4) One "Effects: Full / Light" switch (Light = loops off, bursts halved), and Light turns on by itself if the frame rate stays under 40 for 3 seconds. (5) Every emitter is destroyed with its aura (the same `keepAura` cleanup), so nothing leaks between turns. (6) Slow-mo and zoom go through `setSpeed` and `focusCamera` only, so skipping a cut-in still works.
  - Stone (Earth's Bulwark):
    - Loop: 3 to 4 small rock shards orbiting the waist on a flat ellipse, passing behind and in front (same depth trick as bulwarkAura). Art: stone_burst_0 to 2 cut down small as particle frames, earth_crystal_0 to 3 for the bigger shards that already orbit.
    - Step: a puff of dust at each tile left. Art: dust_ring_0 to 3 (flat, 0.4 scale), plus 4 to 6 brown 'fx-dot' grains.
    - Hit (when a Stone unit lands a blow or takes one): a crack ring on the ground under the target. Art: earth_cast_0 (the ring) laid flat and scaled out fast, crackle_0 to 4 as a quick flipbook on top, plus a few shard particles thrown up and falling with gravity.
  - Swift (Verse of Swiftness):
    - Loop: keep swiftAura (swift_swirl_0 to 5); add a few light wind streak particles drifting sideways. Art: swift_streaks as a particle frame.
    - Move: a wind trail that follows the unit along the whole path (an emitter attached to the unit while it slides, not only at each tile), plus the existing swiftTrail gust at the start. Art: swift_trail_0 to 5, swift_ribbons as particle frames, low alpha.
    - Hit: a small swift_ring flash at the target and a burst of streaks in the blow's direction. Art: swift_ring, swift_streaks.
  - Fury (Verse of Fury):
    - Loop: rising embers from the feet to above the head (replaces the 4 hand-tweened circles in furyAura with one emitter, same colours 0xff7a2e and 0xffb347), plus a heat shimmer: a faint wavy band above the unit. Shimmer is built as a soft fury_column at 15 to 20% opacity with a slow sideways sine wobble and stretch (no shader, so it works on every phone). Art: fury_sparks and 'fx-dot' for embers, fury_column for the shimmer, fury_ground stays as the ring.
    - Step: a few embers left on each tile, fading in about half a second. Art: 'fx-dot', fury_sparks.
    - Hit: a short flame flare at the target. Art: fury_cast_4 to 6 as a fast flipbook, fury_ring flash, ember burst.
  - Slow (Verse of Slowness, and units it slows):
    - Loop: drifting purple motes that sink slowly around the unit (replaces the single tweened slow_motes picture with an emitter; slow_ring stays overhead). Art: slow_motes cut into a few mote frames, 'fx-dot' tinted purple, slow_spiral as a faint slow turn at the feet.
    - Step: the unit leaves a fading slow_waves ripple on the tile (slowed units only).
    - Hit: slow_pull flash inward and motes pulled toward the target. Art: slow_pull, slow_waves.
  - Engagements (clashes):
    - Weapon meet: at each beat's contact moment, clashFlash plus a spark emitter flung both ways (30 particles, gravity, short life) and a white flash card for 2 frames. Art: strike_0 to 6 (fast flipbook at the contact point), sj_impact_0 to 1 for the flash, 'fx-dot' sparks tinted gold.
    - Slow-mo and zoom per beat: on each contact, `setSpeed('slowMo', 0.4)` for about 180 ms and a zoom punch of +6% that eases back, on top of the existing 1.18 / 1.35 beat zoom. Ordinary beats only get the punch; the opening and decisive beats get both.
    - Pulsing ground ring: while a clash is on, a flat ring under the two fighters pulses once per beat and brightens toward the side with momentum. Art: enchant_ring_0 to 3 or sj_rings (pick on the contact sheet at build time), laid flat at 0.36 height like fury_ground.
    - Decisive win: a bigger burst on the loser: sj_burst_0 to 1 and dust_ring at 1.5 scale, 2x sparks, a short camera shake, and the winner's Verse hit effect (above) layered on if they have one.
  - Build order (each step tested before the next): (1) shared helpers: one `fxEmitter` wrapper in src/spellArt.js with the caps, the Full / Light switch and the frame-rate fallback, plus a small cutter if any sheet needs splitting into particle frames; (2) Fury embers and shimmer (simplest, replaces existing tweens); (3) Slow motes; (4) Stone orbit shards; (5) Swift wind trail on the move path; (6) step effects for all four; (7) hit effects for all four; (8) engagement weapon-meet sparks and flash; (9) per-beat slow-mo and zoom punch; (10) pulsing clash ring; (11) decisive burst; (12) phone check: Playwright run at 390 x 844 with 3 Verses and a clash on screen, frame-rate log, screenshots on one contact sheet.

## Phase 17: premium pass (Baiyee 10 Nov; art-director review of one full battle on a 390 x 844 phone)
How it was judged: dev/browser/premium.cjs plays a whole battle with a test player and saves a picture at every key moment (turn start, move, skill menu, attack, clash, hit, death, result). Ranked by how much each one costs the "premium" feel on a phone. "ART" = needs Suleiman; "CODE" = can be built now.
- [x] 17.1 CODE. Figures too small and a third of the screen empty. The fighters stood about 45 pixels tall on the phone, the board sat in the top two thirds and the bottom third was black rock; after every hit the view also jumped back out. Fix: play closer on an upright phone, keep the view in the free space between the turn strip and the cards, and stop the hit jolt resetting the zoom.

No fatigue rule (dropped 13 Nov): only cut dead time (enemy thinking time, slow animations); battle length should come from how well the player plays. Needs Baiyee's pick. Built 14 Nov (M11). MEASURED (the test player in dev/tests/bot.cjs plays the heroes, the game's AI the enemies, cavern): 4 v 4, 12 finished battles: heroes won 1, lost 11; 76 to 132 turns, middle about 110. 3 v 3 the same day: 0 of 6, 57 to 83 turns. 3 v 3 on the 13 Nov code (before today's archer fixes and Kofi): 0 of 8, 57 to 84 turns, so today's work did not cause the imbalance. Where the turns go: the heroes spend most turns locked in clashes (58 stance turns against 62 attacks over 6 battles; the enemies started 77 clashes), and the archer does the most damage. So checklist G1 (20 to 35 turns) FAILS and is not a dead-time problem: it is clash length and balance, for M15 and Baiyee's decision. CUT, dead time only: (1) Enemy turns: Normal or Fast, a pill under the Effects pill, remembered on the device; Fast plays every enemy turn at double speed (tweens and timers; a hero's turn and the result screen stay at normal speed; slow motion and hit-stop still multiply in). (2) The pause before an enemy acts is 0.4 s (it was 0.6). (3) A knockback's wait is divided by the battle speed (it used to wait its full real time at any speed). Timed in a real browser (`node dev/browser/pace.cjs`): a full round of 4 enemies took 6.7 s at Fast at the longest and 12.9 s at Normal, so checklist G5 passes. Default is Normal (my pick: a first-time player should see what the enemies do). Test n63. The bot now prints the middle turn count and every skill and item used. - [x] 18.47 Faster loading (Baiyee and Suleiman 20 Nov: "the game takes too long to start"). Cause: the title scene fetched every picture the game owns (1053 files, 39.6 MB) before "Tap to begin" showed, whatever the player would pick. Built 20 Nov: (1) the title loads nothing (it is words and drawn shapes), so it is ready at once; (2) the character select loads only the portraits (0.5 MB); (3) Start battle loads just the picked fighters' pictures plus the board, effects and HUD pictures every battle needs, behind a loading bar (BattleScene.preload, src/battleLoad.js; who fights comes from lineup.js fieldedEntries, the same list the battle builds its units from, so the two cannot disagree; a second battle in one visit finds everything cached and shows no bar); (4) every PNG in public/art has a WebP twin that the game fetches (lossless in 18.47, lossy at quality 90 since 18.48) (src/artUrl.js; dev/tools/make_webp.py makes and checks them, manifest dev/tools/webp_manifest.json; 1176 pictures, PNG 44.8 MB to WebP 25.6 MB, every pixel pair checked identical). The PNGs stay the masters for the Python tools and the jsdom tests (node-canvas cannot read WebP, so the test browser asks for PNG; a page opened with ?png does too); the build leaves them out of the published game (vite.config.js). Measured with dev/browser/loadtime.cjs (a cold phone, cache off, Playwright network throttle), BEFORE then AFTER: 4G-ish 9 Mbit/s with 170 ms delay: "Tap to begin" at 52.1 s then 1.3 s; battle on screen 60.3 s then 47.1 s. Slow 4G 1.6 Mbit/s with 150 ms: "Tap to begin" at 213.7 s then 3.3 s; battle on screen 222.6 s then 141.6 s (the select is reached at 13 s; the rest is the battle load, 998 files, 19.3 MB: fighters 10.6 MB (8 fighters), effects 5.8 MB, terrain 1.7 MB, HUD 0.8 MB). Not done: fighter-by-fighter effect loading (the effect pictures are not keyed to fighters: a miss would show as a missing picture mid-battle), and fetching the shared battle pictures while the player reads the story and the select (idea: it would cut the Start battle wait by about 8 MB). Test n92 (title loads nothing, select loads portraits, only the picked fighters load, bar shows and goes, second battle fetches nothing, WebP address rule, every PNG has a current twin); resume.cjs H4 now checks the title and the battle's bar. After any cutter writes a PNG: run `~/.fabien-tools/venv/bin/python -I dev/tools/make_webp.py` and commit the .webp files.
- [x] 18.48 Loading part 2 (Baiyee 20 Nov, second message: "the battle still takes 47 s on 9 Mbit/s; get the battle download under 5 MB if you can"). Built 20 Nov: (1) a background download, src/prefetch.js: from the title on it quietly fetches the select's portraits, then the board, effects and HUD pictures every battle uses, then the fighters of the remembered line-up; in the select, newly picked fighters jump to the front; the bytes are kept as blobs that artUrl.js hands to the loader (so it works whatever cache headers the server sends), at most 4 at a time, and Start battle pauses it (the loader takes the rest, with the bar). A browser with no fetch (the jsdom tests) or a page opened with ?png does nothing. (2) The WebP twins are lossy at quality 90 (95 or lossless for a picture where the difference passes a limit; tiny pictures stay lossless): 903 at q90, 4 at q95, 271 lossless; the twins went from 25.6 MB (lossless) to 17.2 MB (PNG masters 45.0 MB). Look checked on a contact sheet, master next to twin, of the 12 pictures with the biggest error (dev/tools/webp_sheet.py; flame glows, the dark fighters, 3x zoom of the worst spot): edges and glows hold, only the busiest flame texture softens a little. The pictures are pixel art, so lossy costs more than on photos: q90 saves a third, not a fifth. (3) The Blender waterfall loops (0.47 MB) are fetched only with ?water=blender. (4) Shrinking: nothing is stored bigger than it is drawn. Measured on the board (phone, desktop 1x and 2x, closest zoom): a fighter's picture is drawn at 0.75 to 1.13 screen pixels per picture pixel on the phone and up to 1.67 on a 2x desktop (more than 1 means the screen shows it larger than the file), and the effects are drawn at about 3 times their stored size (they were already halved in 6.58), so no picture was shrunk. Measured with dev/browser/loadtime.cjs on the same throttled cold phone as 18.47 (DWELL=<seconds> is how long the player stays on the select before pressing Start battle; art only unless said, the code is 1.8 MB and the two music files 5.8 MB come on top). 9 Mbit/s, 170 ms: Start battle pressed at once, the battle is on screen after 29.0 s (18.47: 47.1 s, 19.3 MB fetched; now 11.5 MB); after 20 s at the select 16.2 s (5.4 MB fetched); after 40 s at the select 2.9 s (0.4 MB fetched). 1.6 Mbit/s, 150 ms: at once 100.6 s (18.47: 141.6 s; 12.9 MB fetched); after 60 s at the select 40.5 s (6.4 MB fetched). "Tap to begin" stays at 1.1 to 3.3 s. So the 5 MB target is met for a player who spends about 25 s or more on the story and the select on 9 Mbit/s, and not met for a player who presses at once (about 11 MB of art, the rest has not arrived) or on the slow connection. Why not more: all the art a battle can use is 13.4 MB and the theme music (3.3 MB) and the code (1.8 MB) share the connection while the player reads. Ideas not done: start the battle music file during the story too (2.5 MB), fetch only the poses a first turn needs and the rest after it (a missing picture mid-battle is the risk), recompress the terrain JPEGs (0.6 MB). Test n96 (order, 4 at a time, no repeats, Blender loops skipped, blobs handed out, a changed line-up first, Start battle pauses, a failed download is skipped); n92 and the stale-twin check still apply, and after any cutter run make_webp.py. Suite: the first full run of this batch was 90 of 92 (n56 failed because the Blender loops are no longer fetched, so it now asks for them with window.__loadBlenderWater; tap-circles failed from a flake that was already there: at the previous commit it failed 3 runs of 6). Cause of the flake: the camera works out where a tap is in the world from a matrix that is only refreshed when a frame is drawn, and after the camera settled at zoom 1 it still held the matrix of a nearly-1 zoom, so the tap landed 3 pixels off and hit the enemy instead of the tile; tap-circles and tap-behind now refresh the matrix before each tap (0 failures in 8 and 6 runs). The second full run: 92 of 92, 0 failed, in one run. Browser: battle.cjs both 1 passed (desktop 99 turns, phone 130 turns, 0 page errors); jumps.cjs desktop passed; jumps.cjs phone failed once (H2 moved 1.3 tiles in 80 ms during an attack, turn 35) and passed on the next two runs (about 60 frames a second, 0.7 percent slow): not reproduced, so not explained; resume.cjs passed. Lens: Flow (again): the wait moves to where the player is already reading, so the game does not stop them.

Built 27 Nov (behind "Weapon streaks"): src/weaponStreak.js. dev/tools/weapon_tips.py finds the weapon tip on every strike picture (88 pictures, 11 fighters; the drawn pixel reaching furthest the way he faces, feet left out; the Bull's mace and Tayo's front fists pinned by hand) into src/data/weaponTips.json. On the strike picture the streak runs from his weapon round his shoulder (blades: Sudeen, the Thief, Gadara), along the arm (spears and stabs: Gaha, Rabdul, the archer's dagger) or as a short heavy smear (the Bull's mace, Tayo's fists, Leol's hammer), following the body as it lunges, and lands with the blow; it takes the Verse's colour. The arc round the target (heavySlash) is off when it is on. Alagara keeps Suleiman's own drawn slash (photo 1768). Before and after GIFs: Fabien Blueprint/GRAPHICS 2.0 2026-11-27/weapon streaks. - [x] 18.58 Fighter lighting: a normal map for every fighter picture; torches, braziers and spell effects light the fighters through Phaser's light pipeline, soft and warm. Effects Light turns it off; phones keep a smooth frame rate. Built 27 Nov (behind "Fighter lighting"): src/fighterLight.js. make_normals.py --all made a normal map for all 686 fighter pictures (half size, public/art/normals, WebP twins, 8 MB, downloaded only when the switch is on). Only the fighters take Light2D; the painted board keeps its own light, so the cost is a dozen sprites. Warm torch light with a slow flicker on every fire, a short flash at every hit (fxLayers) and every one of Suleiman's GIF effects (coloured by its name), lightning, and the Fury or Enchant glow. Effects Light (or the frame-rate watcher) turns it off at once.
