## Phase 19: new classes and revised kits (Suleiman's PDFs of 17 to 19 Nov; written down 19 Nov; NOTHING BUILT, Baiyee's instruction: process only)
Seven PDFs arrived (chat files 1639, 1640, 1663, 1672, 1673, 1675, 1676), read with pdf_text.py (ReportLab ones) and a PDF reader for the Chromium one (1676). All numbers are his placeholders. Suleiman 17 Nov 08:45: "have claude make the necessary adjustments to balance them". Stats of Sudeen, Bull Commander and the Archer in the sheets match units.json and jobs.json today. Order Suleiman wants: Alagara and Gadara (Phase 10) first, the slinger next.

### 19.1 Leol, the Slinger (chat file 1639, "Fabien_Leol_Slinger_Class_Framework.pdf", 2 pages, v1.0; sheets 6.76, earlier art 6.67)
- Role: a dual-weapon defensive controller. Long-range support, positional denial and close-range knockback. Core loop: the sling controls distance, the hammer recovers it. A traditional two-cord sling with a leather pouch (the David and Goliath sling, never a Y-shaped slingshot) and a compact construction hammer. Weapon choice changes the available actions, NOT the number of turns, and switching weapons must not give a free second attack.
- Stats and clash profile: NOT GIVEN. Claude decides (the sheet says: switch cost, damage and range, action windows, trigger behaviour, cooldowns, passive, barricade HP and duration and geometry, AI).
- SLING stance: Sling Shot (the default single-target physical ranged attack); Smoke Pellet (a temporary smoke that blocks sight and covers an ally's retreat; reuse the existing smoke effect, smoke.json); Suppressive Fire (mark a threatened area; the first enemy to walk in triggers a prepared sling attack); Concussive Shot (a heavy projectile that damages and can interrupt a prepared action, such as a Poison Arrow aim or Singularity).
- HAMMER stance: Hammer Strike (a close hit that knocks an adjacent enemy back, provisionally 1 tile, with the existing knockback rules); Barricade (he hammers a chosen adjacent tile and a destructible wall rises after ONE TURN; it counts as one occupied tile, blocks walking, physical shots follow the normal cover and line-of-sight rules, enemies can destroy it, any collision reuses the displacement rules; unlike Tayo's temporary spikes).
- Passive, candidates (not locked): Prepared Defender (after a successful hammer knockback his next sling attack gets an accuracy bonus; recommended first) or Steady Hand (holding position improves the next sling attack's range or accuracy; the simpler fallback). Do not stack both. Clash signature: none given.
- Expected play: an enemy far away, sling stance with Smoke Pellet or Suppressive Fire; an enemy about to act, Concussive Shot; an enemy reaches him, hammer stance and Hammer Strike; an ally needs cover, Barricade.
- Does it change anything built? No, it is a new class. It needs: a weapon switch (the archer's bow and dagger switch, readyCloseWeapons, is the model), an overwatch trigger (shared with Rabdul's Spear Ward, 19.4), a destructible obstacle with health (digsites already block a tile), and a way to interrupt a prepared action.
- Open for Suleiman: his stats, whether he is a hero or an enemy (6.61 says the heroes lack ranged damage, which he fits), the clash profile.
- [x] BUILT 26 Nov (parts 6 and 7 of the 22 Nov batch). A hero (h6), benched by default (pick him in the character select; he can fight on either side). Numbers picked by Claude: health 32, attack 6, defense 3, magic attack 1, magic defense 2, accuracy 88, evade 12, speed 9, move 3, sling range 2 to 4, the hammer on the next tile (attack +1). The archer's free weapon switch (once a turn, never a second attack); a sling skill takes the sling, a hammer skill the hammer. Sling Shot: a stone on a low arc. Smoke Pellet: himself or an ally up to 4 tiles, Hidden until their next turn, foes next to them Blinded, cooldown 3. Suppressive Fire: Rabdul's ward at sling reach (2 to 4 tiles, in clear sight), one full-blow shot, a hit stops the walker, he cannot move after, cooldown 3. Concussive Shot: 2 to 4 tiles, a full blow, a hit breaks Singularity, an aim, a ward or a meditation, cooldown 3. Hammer Strike: a full blow and a 1 tile push (usual push rules), cooldown 2. Barricade: an empty tile next to him, the wall rises at the start of his next turn, 12 points, 3 of his turns, blocks walking and shots; a foe with nobody to attack breaks it down; cooldown 4. Passive Prepared Defender: after a push that moves the foe, +15 to his next sling shot. Skills grouped Sling and Hammer. Pictures: 13 sheets cut (stand, battle, battle-worn, hurt, sling dodge, hammer guard, hammer strike, hammer raise, sling shot, victory, defeated) plus the barricade; faces from 21 Nov (neutral, hurt, angry). No walk sheet (code walk). Test n116; bot battles on both sides.

### 19.2 The Thief, the Infiltrator (chat file 1640, "Fabien_Thief_Class_Framework.pdf", 2 pages, v1.0; sheets 6.77)
- Role: anti-positioning specialist and mage hunter. Core loop: Hide, reposition, exploit, expose, regain Hide. Concealment gives covert movement, traps and sudden big damage, at the cost of being revealed; high ground is the counter.
- Stats and clash profile: NOT GIVEN. Claude decides: movement numbers, how elevation is compared on ties and on flat maps, trap visibility, persistence and cap, damage and durations, action costs, status compatibility, knockback resistance, AI.
- Hide (passive): ending a turn without taking an action makes him Hidden. Hidden Movement (passive): while Hidden he travels more tiles than normal, and moving alone does not reveal him. Ambush (Hidden only): a close-range burst of damage plus Armor Weakening plus Bleeding, and it ALWAYS reveals him, hit or miss. Trap (Hidden only): plant an explosive on a tile, he must stay put for one turn; it fires when an enemy enters the tile (moderate damage, base 2 tile knockback away from the trigger, a ledge means fall damage); it stays active whatever he does later.
- Statuses: Bleeding (damage every turn; any healing item removes it); Armor Weakening (defense down for a while; size and length not given); Detection (an enemy standing at the highest elevation on the battlefield can expose a Hidden Thief); he can hide again by meeting the normal end-turn rule.
- Proposal, not settled: tell resisting a push (a sturdy unit) from softening a fall (an agile unit). Add it only if the existing knockback, Rooted, collision, Bulwark and elevation rules are not enough; the sheet wants one global rule, not a Thief exception.
- Matchups: strong against ranged and mage units, weak to high-ground scouts and mobile defenders.
- Does it change anything built? No, a new class. Hidden exists today only as the archer's Smoke Bomb (untargetable for one turn); this Hidden lasts until he is revealed. Bleeding, Armor Weakening, Detection and a persistent Trap are new. Poison and its cures (Herbs, Bitter leaf) are the model for Bleeding.
- Open for Suleiman: stats, side (hero or enemy), the female variant (he said he will draw one, voice 1674).
- [x] BUILT 26 Nov. An enemy (e6), benched by default (pick him in the character select; either side). Numbers picked by Claude: health 26, attack 7, defense 2, accuracy 92, evade 20, speed 12, move 4, range 1. Hide: ending a turn without acting (and not locked in a clash) makes him Hidden until he attacks, uses Ambush, is hit, or is spotted. Hidden Movement: +2 move while Hidden. Ambush (Hidden only): 1.5 times a blow, +10 to hit from hiding, defense -2 (Armor Weakening, until the foe's next turn ends), Bleeding 2 a turn for 3 turns (shown as BLEEDING; any healing item stops it), always reveals him, cooldown 2. Trap (Hidden only): an empty tile next to him, he cannot walk after, unseen by the other side, a foe walking in takes 6 and is thrown 2 tiles back the way it came (slams and falls as usual), 2 at most (the oldest goes), does not reveal him, cooldown 2. Detection: a foe whose turn starts on the map's highest ground, within 5 tiles and in clear sight, spots him. His AI hunts casters and archers first, plants traps when foes are near, and hides when no kill is on. The push proposal (sturdy versus agile) is not built: the existing rules were enough. Pictures: 13 sheets cut (stand, battle, battle-worn, hurt, slash, dodge, parry, ambush leap, laying a trap, steal, victory, fallen, defeated) and his trap explosion GIF; 15 faces from 21 Nov (neutral, hurt, angry wired). Test n116; bot battles on both sides.

### 19.3 Sudeen's kit (chat file 1663, "Sudeen_Kit_Sheet.pdf", 3 pages, Draft, 18 Nov 08:49)
- Role: the Warden, a Guardian, Defence style. Holds the line, shares damage with allies beside him, pulls friends out of danger. Modest offence. Stats: Speed 9, Move 3, HP 48, Attack 6, Defense 4, Magic attack 1, Magic defense 2, Accuracy 85, Evade 10, Reach 1. Clash: Power 4, Guard 8, Agility 3; Press average, Guard strong, Slip weak. Items: Shea balm and Kola nut. Verse of Stone is doubled on him (Earth's Bulwark gives +4 defense). Same numbers as today.
- Ward (passive, his signature): allies on the tiles next to him get +1 defense (+2 under Stone), and he takes one third of each hit that lands on them (rounded up, at most 4; under Stone half and at most 5), worked out after defense. It is armor, not a buff, so Layra's Verses stack with it and Sunder Boon and Dominion cannot remove it. He cannot share below 1 health, so he cannot die through an ally. Only hits are shared (not poison, burning, falls or slams). Off when he is Stunned or at 1 health. Example: attack 8 against Rabdul (defense 3, so 4 under Ward): the hit is 4, Sudeen takes 2 and Rabdul takes 2 (it would have been 5). The sheet says it stays on in a clash; HIS NEWER NOTES say off in a clash and switchable (13.68).
- Shield Bash (normal attack, no cooldown): reach 1, attack minus defense (at least 1), on a hit 50 percent chance of Disoriented (2 damage on impact, attack -2, defense -2, evade -10, magic does +3 to it, through its next turn). The Disorient effect has a 2 turn cooldown that starts only when Disoriented lands (a failed roll does not start it; while it cools the bash is plain damage). No roll on an already Disoriented target. Optional: +20 percent (70 in all) against a Distracted or Off-Balance target.
- Intercept, enemy mode (3 turns, range 4): charge in for half damage and lock the foe in a clash; if the foe was clashing with an ally he takes it over and the ally goes free. As built. Intercept, ally mode (NEW, 2 turns, ally within 4): swap tiles with the ally; if the ally is in a clash he takes it over and the ally is free; no damage; needs a clear path and the ally's tile within 1 level of his. Variants (3 MP lent by Layra): Shield Ram (Stone: half damage, push 2, no clash, a slam does 2 damage and Disoriented), Furious Charge (Fury: full damage, no clash), Blitz (Swiftness: half damage and Stun, no clash). As built. Herbs (3 turns, 3 pouches, ally within 2, +10 health, cures poison and burn). As built.
- How he plays: holds a clash with Guard 8 while allies behind him are covered (the cost: Distracted, others hit him at +10); Relay Strike keeps allies standing; Rescue with ally Intercept (an aimed Poison Arrow); Shield Bash disorients where the Bull's toss stuns. Weak to lightning and ranged attacks (Evade 10, magic defense 2), Slip beats Guard, Stun turns Ward off. An enemy AI rule for focus-fire on isolated units may be needed.
- Open questions on the sheet: Ward on adjacent tiles only or within 2; a one third share (max 4) or a flat 2; ally Intercept 2 or 3 turns; 50 or 40 percent Disorient; the +20 percent bonus; whether the Hold rule keeps Ward at half strength within 2 tiles when he ends a turn without acting.
- [x] BUILT 21 Nov (Batch 7; Suleiman and Baiyee agreed 21 Nov that his strength should show in clashes, where he does most of his damage). CLASH: he had no clash signature at all (the Bulwark signature in engagement.json belonged to an unused "warden" job), so a clash gave him nothing of his own. Now his job has Bulwark (Guard): each beat in a row in Guard adds a level (up to 3, 1 tile less pushed each, an even Guard beat gives 2 health and 1 Poise back, as written for the Warden), and NEW, a beat he WINS with Guard lands a Shield Bash of 2 + his level (3, then 4, then 5; engagement.json signatures.bulwark.winHitBase), on top of the decisive strike. WARD (passive, numbers as the sheet): allies on the 4 tiles next to him +1 defense (+2 under Stone, armour, so it stacks with Verses and Dominion cannot take it); he takes a third of each hit on them, rounded up, at most 4 (under Stone half, at most 5), worked out after defense, never below 1 health; only hits (landHit), not poison, burns or falls; off in a clash (13.68), Stunned, at 1 health, or switched off. A blue line flashes from the ally to him with "Ward -n". SHIELD BASH (his Attack, renamed): on a hit 50 percent to Disorient (70 against an Off-Balance or Distracted foe), with 2 more damage when it lands; not on a foe already Disoriented; then 2 of his turns before it can again (the wait starts only when it lands). INTERCEPT ALLY (new skill, the sheet's ally mode): swap tiles with an ally up to 4 walking steps away (a clear path, its tile at most 1 level from his); if the ally is in a clash he takes it over ("Locked in!", the ally "Free!") and his turn ends; no damage; ready again after 2 turns (own cooldown; enemy-mode Intercept keeps its 3). His AI and the test player use it on an ally in a clash at half health or less. The sheet's open questions answered with its first numbers (adjacent only, a third max 4, ally mode 2 turns, 50 percent plus the 20 bonus); the Hold rule is not built. Test n108.
- Does it change anything built? Mostly new: Ward, Shield Bash and Intercept's ally mode are new; everything else is already in the game (Intercept, its three variants, Herbs, Disoriented, Stun, the clash take-over). Today Sudeen has only Herbs and Intercept. Suleiman 16 Nov 21:19: "excited for [Sudeen's?] summons to come into play... the spirits will complete him" (probably Kofi's spirits, see questions).

### 19.4 Rabdul, revised kit (chat file 1672, "Rabdul_Revised_Kit_Sheet.pdf", 4 pages, 18 Nov 22:18)
- Role: a fast, evasive, fragile fighter who controls movement with a double-ended spear (Twin Spear, Overpower style): denies approaches, sets up allies' Relay hits, switches between mobile offence and a stationary defensive posture. Stats (as today): Speed 9, Move 3, HP 34, Attack 7, Defense 3, Magic attack 1, Magic defense 2, Accuracy 88, Evade 14, Reach 1, MP 9. Clash: Power 6, Guard 5, Agility 5, Press strong. Start item Kola nut.
- Built today (matches the sheet): Double Hit (normal attack, 35 percent for two hits at 60 percent each, each rolled alone; to test: does Enchant apply to each hit or once per attack) and Enchant (3 MP, cooldown 3, range 3, 3 turns, +2 damage per hit, +1 against Drenched).
- NEW passives: Switch Ends (a later hit on the same enemy in his turn may give a small bonus, TBD; in a clash a winning Press beat adds a rear-blade strike, baseline 2 damage; no endless chains) and No Blind Side (keeps the clash Distracted reduction, +10 cut to +5, provisionally; any extra defense against adjacent reactions must be tested first). Haft Sweep (active, cooldown 3, reach 1, half damage, Off-Balance and a push of 1 tile, 2 downhill; sets up an allied Relay).
- NEW actives: Spear Ward (replaces Whirl; active, cooldown 3): overwatch until the start of his next turn. It threatens all 8 tiles around him; when an enemy ENTERS one (an enemy already standing next to him does not trigger it) a reaction attack at 75 percent damage rolls normally; a hit stops the enemy on that tile for the rest of its walk (it may still act); a miss uses a charge but does not stop it; two charges in all; he cannot move while it holds; no stun and no lost action. Hold the Line (replaces Plant and Vault; active, cooldown 3): he braces on his tile until his next turn: a modest Guard bonus (value TBD), resists pushes and pulls, and once may contest an enemy trying to walk past him (a successful contest stops it); he cannot move; not immunity. One stance at a time: using one replaces the other.
- Verse variants (new, Whirl's old ones were retired): Swiftness: Rapid Ward (one limited move before the ward starts; two reaction cap; no automatic hits). Stone: Anchored Ward (a successful intercept may push the enemy 1 tile back; he is Rooted until next turn). Fury: Kindle the Haft (an Enchant variant: self-Enchant; his first successful hit each turn ignites the target for 2 damage a turn for 3 turns; Drenched targets do not ignite and get the +1 instead). Variant cost 3 MP; shares the base skill's cooldown.
- Counters: Health 34, Defense 3, Magic defense 2; two reactions can be used up, ranged and magic ignore the zone.
- Open questions: how often two intercepts may fire (a safeguard: one reaction per enemy movement); can a hit stop a heavily armoured unit; how much Guard Hold the Line gives; are the two stances too alike; how Enchant and Double Hit stack; whether the 3 MP variant cost replaces or adds to the move cost; what a successful contest means (use clash and accuracy).
- [x] BUILT 21 Nov (Batch 7). SPEAR WARD (active, cooldown 3): until the start of his next turn the 8 tiles round him are guarded (lit faintly on the floor; SPEAR WARD 2 over him); a foe that walks INTO one (from a tile that was not) is struck for 75 percent of a blow with a normal roll; a hit stops its walk on that tile (its steps are gone; it may still act), a miss uses the strike and lets it walk on; 2 strikes in all; each foe is struck at most once per walk (the sheet's safeguard); he cannot move after setting it; it is off while he is in a clash or Stunned; ranged and magic ignore it. Built as a check in moveUnit, so it works on any walk, hero or enemy (a hero's walk-and-strike that is stopped short keeps its turn and strikes nothing). HOLD THE LINE (active, cooldown 3): +2 Guard in a clash (the sheet's value TBD, picked), no push or pull moves him, and the first foe that walks next to him is contested with his hit chance (a win stops it, no damage); he cannot move. One stance at a time: using one replaces the other. HAFT SWEEP (active, cooldown 3, reach 1): half a blow, Off-Balance, pushed 1 tile (further down a drop, a slam into a wall), allies next to where it lands may Relay. SWITCH ENDS (passive, his clash signature, Press): a Press beat he wins brings the rear blade round for 2 (engagement.json signatures.switchEnds); the second blow of his Double Hit does 1 more. NO BLIND SIDE (passive): caught in someone else's clash, attackers get +5 to hit him, not +10. VERSE FORMS (3 MP, Layra's Verses): Swiftness Rapid Ward (set the ward and still walk up to 2 tiles; the ward goes with him), Stone Anchored Ward (a strike that hits also pushes the foe 1 tile; he is Rooted until his next turn), Fury Kindle the Haft (Enchant on his own spear: +2 a hit for 3 turns, and his first hit each turn sets the foe alight, 2 a turn for 3 turns; a Drenched foe takes 1 more instead). His AI: Haft Sweep when the push drops the foe a level, slams it or lands it next to one of his allies (else now and then); with nobody next to him but a foe who could reach him next turn, Spear Ward (or its Verse form), Hold the Line while it cools. Open questions answered: one strike per foe per walk; a hit stops even a heavy fighter; the contest is his hit chance; Enchant's bonus is on each hit of the Double Hit (as built). Test n109.
- Does it change anything built? Not in the game: only Double Hit and Enchant are in jobs.json for him; Whirl and Plant and Vault were never built (the only "whirl" in the game is Gaha's Sonic Javelin). So no code is retired. All the rest is new and needs a reaction system (overwatch with charges) shared with Leol's Suppressive Fire (19.1).

### 19.5 Bull Commander, rebalanced (chat file 1673, "Bull_Commander_Rebalanced_Kit_Sheet.pdf", 2 pages, v2, 19 Nov 03:13; art 6.73)
- Goal: fewer overlapping crowd-control tools. Only TWO actives remain. Stats unchanged: Speed 8, Move 3, HP 48, Attack 8, Defense 3, Magic 1 and 1, Accuracy 85, Evade 8, Reach 1; clash Power 8, Guard 4, Agility 3 (Press strong; Guard and Slip weak); items Shea balm and Kola nut. Suleiman 16 Nov 21:19: fights will be faster but worse "if you're not careful".
- Mace Strike: normal attack, reach 1, attack 8, no added stun or conditional bonus.
- Horn Charge (active, 3 turn cooldown): charge in a straight, unobstructed line 2 to 3 tiles and strike the first enemy; cannot pass through units or climb more than 1 level; no clash. A 2 tile charge deals 75 percent damage, a 3 tile charge 100 percent. On a hit choose ONE outcome: push a non-Guarding target 1 tile (2 downhill), or put a Guarding target Off-Balance instead; never both. If an enemy is actually pushed into impassable ground, the old 2 collision damage plus Disoriented applies; a Guarding target that is not pushed cannot trigger it. A miss does nothing and gives no free follow-up.
- Shield Toss (active, 3 turn cooldown): range 3, half damage, a higher block stops it. It Stuns for 1 turn ONLY if the target was already Off-Balance before the toss; otherwise damage only; it never creates Off-Balance itself; no guaranteed hit.
- Bloodied Press (clash signature, unchanged): each hit he takes adds a Bloodied stack, up to 3; during Press each stack cuts every incoming hit by 1; winning a Press beat lands a hit of 1 plus his stacks, a decisive win pushes 1 more tile. Stacks come only from real hits.
- Shield Toss Verse variants (Layra lends 3 MP; share the 3 turn cooldown; no automatic hits): Swiftness, Ricochet Toss (half damage to the first target, then half to a second enemy within 2 tiles of it; only the first may be Stunned, and only if already Off-Balance; separate hit checks); Fury, Wrath Toss (full damage and Off-Balance on a hit instead of Stun; no free Bloodied stack); Stone, Quake Toss (half damage to the main target, Stun if it was already Off-Balance; on a hit, other enemies next to the target are pushed 1 tile away where legal; no stun for them).
- Removed: Shatter Guard (its Off-Balance job is folded into Horn Charge against a Guarding target) and Rally Cry (no cleanse, no Poise, no charge meter).
- Loop: find a lane, charge 2 to 3 tiles, shove an exposed foe or unbalance a Guarding one, let allies use the opening, keep Shield Toss for an Off-Balance target; in a clash Bloodied Press is his strength. Weak: Evade 8, magic defense 1, straight line and a 2 tile runway, a charge can be baited onto a non-Guarding target, Slip and Guard are weak clash choices.
- [x] BUILT 21 Nov (Batch 7, with 6.73). HORN CHARGE (active, cooldown 3): a straight, clear lane of 2 or 3 tiles (no one in the way, no step up of more than 1 level) into the first foe, no clash; 75 percent of a blow from 2 tiles, a full blow from 3; a hit pushes the foe 1 tile (further down a drop; into a wall the slam, 2 damage and Disoriented), or knocks a Guarding foe (Guard in a clash, Rooted, or Rabdul's Hold the Line) Off-Balance instead, never both; a miss does nothing; allies may Relay a pushed foe. He runs the lane in his charge pictures with after-images and dust, and the sweep of dust (or fire under Enchant) plays at the impact. His AI charges before walking whenever a lane is open (the longest first, a foe it can push, then a caster, then the weakest); the test player does too. SHIELD TOSS: the Stun only on a foe that was already Off-Balance (else damage only; it never makes Off-Balance); his AI now throws first at an Off-Balance foe (even with someone in mace reach), so Horn Charge on a Guarding foe sets up the Stun. VERSE FORMS (3 MP, the shared cooldown): Swiftness Ricochet Toss (half to the target, then half to another foe within 2 tiles of it, each rolled; only the first can be Stunned), Fury Wrath Toss (a full blow and Off-Balance on a hit, no Stun), Stone Quake Toss (half, Stun if already Off-Balance, and on a hit the other foes next to the target are thrown 1 tile away). Mace Strike and Bloodied Press unchanged; Shatter Guard and Rally Cry never existed. Test n110.
- Does it change anything built? Yes, two things. (1) Shield Toss today stuns every target; the sheet makes the stun conditional on Off-Balance, so its text and numbers change. (2) None of Horn Charge, the three toss variants, Shatter Guard or Rally Cry exists in jobs.json or spells.json, so removing the last two changes nothing. His AI (enemyAttack) must learn Horn Charge. Bloodied Press is unchanged. Test priorities on the sheet: charge distance damage, collisions, Stun and Off-Balance timing, Relay chains, Bloodied Press with Fury.

### 19.6 The Archer, Ranger (chat file 1675, "Archer_Ranger_Kit_Sheet.pdf", 2 pages, design review 19 Nov; source cited: docs/BATTLE_SYSTEMS.md on GitHub)
- Role: a fast, fragile precision fighter who controls distance, prepares poison shots and escapes pressure. Stats, all as in the game: Speed 11, Move 3, Health 29, Attack 5, Defense 1, Magic attack 1, Magic defense 3, Accuracy 90, Evade 15, bow reach 2 to 5. Clash: Evasion style, Power 2, Guard 3, Agility 5; Press weak, Guard weak, Slip average.
- Established: Arrow (range 2 to 5, no bow next to a foe) and Dagger (adjacent only, attack -2); the weapon swap is free once a turn (readyCloseWeapons); Poison Arrow (range 5, aiming uses the whole turn, fires at the start of her next turn if the target is still in reach and in sight; half damage and Poisoned 3 a turn for 3 turns, never the last point; cooldown 3); Smoke Bomb (not in a clash; blinds adjacent enemies 1 turn, then she moves up to 2 tiles and is Hidden 1 turn; cooldown 3). Statuses as built: Hidden, Blinded (hit chance -45, min 5 percent, cannot start a clash or aim), Poisoned. In a clash she cannot shoot or walk; the AI tries to break away 50 percent of the time unless winning.
- NEW, proposed third active: Pinning Shot: bow only, range 2 to 5 with normal sight and accuracy rules; on a hit the target is PINNED until the start of its next turn (cannot choose to move; may attack, use skills and defend in a clash); half bow damage; cooldown 3 (both provisional). Counters: sight blockers, closing in, high evade, foes already in range. The Pinned status does not exist; its rules against forced movement, clashes and cleansing must be settled (shares the Pin and Anchor question of 13.61).
- NEW, proposed passive: Patient Hunter: +5 accuracy on a bow attack if she did not move that turn (test it against the delayed Poison Arrow release; no double count). The alternative, Close-Quarters Reflex (auto-draw the dagger when an adjacent threat blocks the bow), is not additive and probably unneeded; her free swap and her AI already do it. Recommendation on the sheet: test Patient Hunter only.
- Test cases: can she use Pinning Shot and Smoke Bomb without melee units being unable to reach her; is the prepared Poison Arrow risky enough against fast foes; is Patient Hunter too much on top of Accuracy 90; can a Pinned enemy still be pushed or teleported; does dagger switching work at adjacency before and after Smoke Bomb.
- Does it change anything built? No, everything under "established" is already in the game; Pinning Shot, Pinned and Patient Hunter are additions. It applies to the generic archer class and so to the male archer too when he joins the heroes (6.61).

These replace any earlier guess about what a spirit does; the spirit's numbers are still the sheet's placeholders.
  - 12 Nov, Kofi's kit (Suleiman's Kofi_Ability_Sheet.pdf, 3 pages, a draft, file 1396 at 07:32; all numbers are placeholders). Each turn Kofi reads the area within 2 tiles of him. The element with the most points is his dominant element: it sets his spirit's form and the element of his skills that turn. Second element adds a trait. A tie is his choice. A clear day on bare ground gives a weak reading.
  - Haunt (damage over time): 2 MP. Places a spirit mark that ticks for 3 Beats. Range: not on the sheet. Tick by dominant element: Fire = Burn (damage each Beat, spreads to dry grass); Water = Drench (slows movement, makes the target Wet); Earth = Bind (damage each Beat, movement -1); Storm = Shock (a chance to break the target's channel or reaction). Spirit: no spirit needed; uses the dominant element.
  - Spirit Bolt (offensive): 2 MP. A burst of damage at one target, strength scales with the dominant element's points. Range: not on the sheet. Spirit: no spirit needed. Effect by element: Fire ignites flammable tiles; Water sets up a lightning combo; Earth displaces the target 1 tile; Storm chains through wet units and tiles.
  - Call Spirit (summon): 3 MP. Raises a spirit on an adjacent tile (range 1). Form and stats come from the reading: Fire = Ember, Water = Tide, Earth = Stone, Storm = Gale. Integrity is 3 plus the dominant points, so a strong reading gives a strong spirit. Swap: once per Beat, free, Kofi trades places with the spirit. If he is engaged, the spirit takes over the engagement. One spirit at a time; it fades if Kofi falls.
  - Infuse: 2 MP, plus 1 MP and 1 A if no spirit is out. Range: an ally within 3 tiles. The spirit merges into the ally and gives the blessing for the dominant element: Earth = Stone (Guard and Anchor bonus, displacement resistance); Storm = Swiftness (Slip bonus, a free one-tile step); Fire = Fury (Press bonus; Guard -1); Water = Renewal (heals about 5% of max HP each Beat and clears one weak status each Beat). Lasts 3 Beats or until recalled. Integrity drains when the host takes damage. Self-infusion at half strength. Counterspell, Hex or a dispel ends it.
  - Free each turn (not a button): Reading. Lock the current spirit form for 1 MP.
  - Not on the sheet, to ask Suleiman: the range of Haunt and Spirit Bolt; what the 1 A in Infuse's extra cost means; whether the spirit's form locks when summoned or re-reads every turn (the sheet asks this too); a class name for jobs.json; the Renewal look (soft green-gold, not designed yet). The sheet also asks for a cultural consultant to review the class name and the spirit presentation (Yoruba spirit work) before release.

- [x] 13.40 Relay Strike (Suleiman's spec, passed on by Baiyee 10 Nov; the roadmap had no entry, so this one is new). When a foe is pushed, pulled or shoved and stops next to an ally of the pusher, that ally gets a free hit (a reaction): 60 percent damage, full if the foe is Off-Balance; one per ally per round (a full cycle of turns), two at most per push with the second at half strength; not if the ally is in a clash, stunned, meditating, or cannot reach the foe (a bow at point blank); a push toward an ally never harms it. - [x] 13.47 Knockback can land a unit in the gap between blocks (Suleiman 13 Nov 10:12, voice note 1417). Find the cause (landing tile or position after push), then fix. Test with a push from the cavern edges. Built 13 Nov: cause found with the bot battles (a new OFF TILE check in bot.cjs caught it 11 times in 4 battles). It was not the push itself: the small recoil of a hit and the sidestep of a miss started from wherever the body was at that instant and came back to that spot, so a unit hit while still flying from a push, getting up, or lunging in its own attack stayed between two tiles. Fix (BattleScene.js nudgeFromTile): recoil and sidestep are measured from the middle of the unit's tile, worked out again every frame; Relay Strike and Double Hit return to the tile too; settleBodies puts any stray body on its tile at the end of every turn. Test: dev/tests/n53.cjs (cavern): 1,944 pushes from every tile in 8 directions, 1 to 3 tiles (768 from the edge, every height, 595 falls, 724 slams), 659 drawn to the end, plus the 5 ways the bug happened. It fails on the old code. 4 bot battles after the fix: 0 off tile.

Cause not found; find it before fixing. 15 Nov: 17 times in 40 battles on the M6 and M7 code, so it is not rare at speed; next thing to chase. Built 19 Nov. CAUSE FOUND with a per-frame move history in the bot (dev/tests/bot.cjs prints each figure's last moves with any OFF TILE): the attack lunge (playAttack), both clash lunges and Rabdul's Double Hit tweened the body to fixed points measured from where it stood when they began. Pushed to another tile mid-swing (a counter, a Sonic Burst, two pushes at once), the last tween dragged it back to the old tile (the 56 to 84 px cases); begun a little off, or with two of these overlapping (Rabdul's two heads), it came back off the middle (the 10 to 30 px cases). FIX: all of them now move by offsets from the middle of the tile the fighter stands on now, every frame (the 13.47 nudgeFromTile pattern), and leave the body alone while a push draws it. 18 bot battles after: OFF TILE 0 (34 in the 18 before). Test n81 (pushed mid-attack at three moments, an attack begun 12 px off, Double Hit, a clash opening); it fails on the old code with 66 px.

Today every enemy follows the same rules in enemyAttack and enemyAi.js (nearest foe, first skill that fits). Wanted: each fighter has its own personality that changes who it targets, when it uses its skills, how it moves and when it retreats. It is an Opus design job: see docs/NEXT.md. BUILT 27 Nov (with 13.77 and 13.80; Baiyee 27 Nov: build it). Each fighter has a play style in data, src/data/aiStyles.json (by unit id): aggressive (Gaha, Alagara, Tayo, the Bull, Gadara), protector (Sudeen, Rabdul), support (Layra, Kofi), skirmisher (Leol, the Archer, the Thief). A style has careAt (how hurt a friend must be before it heals, guards or saves it), fallBackAt (it walks back to a healer friend when hurt; 0 = never) and focus (weakest, threat, casters). src/aiStyle.js: everyone takes a kill first, then an opening, then its focus (a protector strikes the foe hurting a hurt friend; a skirmisher casters and archers); a support walks into reach of the friend who needs it most, keeping 2 tiles from foes; a protector walks at the foe hurting a friend (Rabdul still keeps with the line, 10.31); a hurt fighter falls back only when a support friend is alive to heal it. Works on either side. PICKED: one style per character (not per class), fixed for the battle (no enrage yet), not shown on the card yet. Measuring: bot.cjs AI_BOTH=1 lets the game's AI play both sides; dev/tests/kit_use.sh runs three line-ups (the 8, the 8 swapped, kings and new classes) and prints each fighter's skills per 10 turns and what it never used. The ground (natureAt, one rule for both): a lamp's tile or next to one = fire, a water tile = water, sand or rubble = earth, 2 levels up or more = storm, plain floor = nothing (Kofi then uses his Reading). Spirits: Ember (fire) Ember Lash, 4 damage and Burn to each foe next to it; Tide (water) Tide Mend, +5 health and one weak status lifted for each ally next to it, Kofi too; Stone (earth) Stone Ward, allies next to it take 2 less a hit (never below 1), 14 points to break; Gale (storm) Gale Burst, 2 damage and a 1 tile push to each foe next to it. All: 3 MP, a tile up to 3 away, ready again after 3 turns, 8 points to break (Stone 14), drawn at 0.62 of Kofi's height from Suleiman's four cut spirits (art.json sp_*, "summonOf": "h4" loads them with Kofi), one move when it comes and at the start of his next 2 turns, then it fades; it blocks its tile, a foe with nobody else to strike breaks it down, it goes when he falls, one at a time. Spirit Swap (6.49): free, once a turn, not in a clash. Picking Call Spirit lights each tile in its spirit's colour and the hint names the spirit. Kofi's card shows TIDE SPIRIT 2 and so on. AI: the tile where the spirit would do most (Ember 4 a foe beside it, Tide the health it gives, Stone 3 a friend in the fight, Gale 3 a foe, +2 if that foe is on a friend; only at 4 or more), Haunt keeps 3 MP back for it; the free swap when a foe is next to him and none next to the spirit. Alagara's Claim from nature: his own tile or an empty tile next to him, the ground's Verse (lamp Fury, water Renewal, sand or rubble Earth's Bulwark, high ground Swiftness) for 2 turns, his action, no MP, the same cooldown; he can still take a foe's or friend's blessing; his AI claims from the ground when he has no Verse (or its last turn): Renewal when hurt, Fury with a foe beside him, else Swiftness, else what is there. Not built: players cannot strike a spirit with their own attack (only the AI breaks one); spirits are not kept in the save (they vanish on reload). Tests n124, n62, n98 (Infuse checks moved to Call Spirit). Kit use (AI both sides, 18 battles): Call Spirit 2.4 and Haunt 2.7 per 10 Kofi turns, Claim 2.9 per 10 Alagara turns.

Built 28 Nov (src/spirits.js): (1) Attack lights a foe's spirit in reach like a fighter (red tile); the first tap shows the blow ('Strike the Stone Spirit: 8 damage, 6 left of 14'), the second strikes. A blow on a spirit is the striker's attack, at least 1 (a spirit has no defense); it is the striker's action; at 0 it breaks. Archers and casters reach it by their own reach rules. (2) The AI (spiritToStrike), before its clashes and skills: a spirit it can break with this blow whose next move is worth 4 or more (Ember 4 and Gale 3 for each of its side next to it, Tide the health it would give, Stone 3 for each guarded friend in a fight), unless a foe in reach can be finished instead; with nobody else in reach it strikes any spirit in reach. (3) The clash take-over (Kofi's 12 Nov sheet: 'If he is engaged, the spirit takes over the engagement'): Spirit Swap now works in a clash (in the clash menu, beside Empower). Kofi is out of the clash on the spirit's tile; the foe is HELD by the spirit on his old tile: no walking or moving skills, no new clash, its plain attack can only strike the spirit; it is free when the spirit breaks, fades, Kofi falls, or the spirit is swapped away. His AI swaps out of any clash when his spirit is out. Card tag HELD, Move button 'Held: no move'. (4) The save keeps every spirit (kind, owner, tile, health, moves left) and the HELD foes; Continue puts them back. Test n126.

## Skeleton animation
Recommendation: frame animation from his sheets now (free); a Gaha skeleton built in code as a test once he makes a parts sheet (prompt written in the doc's skeleton section, 29 Oct); Spine's free trial only after that (Essential $69, Professional $379 one-time; meshes need Professional).
- [x] S.1 Skeleton test (live at /skeleton-test/, public/skeleton-test: rig.json, index.html moves + two-bone solver for feet and front hand, notes.html findings): Suleiman's Gaha parts sheet arrived 29 Oct 20:20 (0774ac65-image.jpg in attachments): head x4 views, neck, torso front/back, arms and legs as outer/inner pieces, hands, feet, hips front/back, cape back + mantle, skirt front/back, spear. Rig Gaha in code, breathe + thrust, beside the frame-by-frame thrust from his sheet on a test page. Findings: clean joins, smooth, ~80 KB per view vs ~1.3 MB per move in 4 directions; but paper-puppet (no body twist), front view only (needs three-quarter parts sheets), stiff one-piece cape/skirt (cut into strips), knee joint mid-calf, limbs drawn smaller. Recommendation: frames for big moves, skeleton for idle/small reactions once three-quarter parts sheets exist.

- [x] S.2 Skeleton test round two (live 30 Oct, commit 430ff08): rig_dr.json (front 3/4, his first pair of sheets: 0ddfc8d2-image.png) and rig_ur.json (back 3/4: 7b88005d-image.png); his second pair (731ef684, 0a04980c) unused. Page buttons: Down-right, Up-right, Round 1, Face left (mirror = all four directions); rig files now hold moves (thrust keys with world angles), ik (legs, grip), sway (spring strips), alts (turned chest/head). Frames: row 1 (down-right), row 2 (up-right). Findings: good for breathing, flinches, walking; twist is a two-picture jump; signature moves still best frame by frame. Sheet problems: thighs as whole legs (cut at the knee), shins with feet, parts about 2x the reference size; assembled by eye against the reference. ~60 KB per view at game size.
- [ ] S.3 Walking and breathing from the skeleton in battle, instead of hopping tile to tile (Suleiman 30 Oct 16:30: "walking, and breathing are useful"). Needs: frames walking towards the camera, and the right leg stepping (ChatGPT keeps drawing the left leg forward; his voice note 19:36). Proposal: frame-by-frame walk from his isometric prompt, about 6 frames facing down-right and 6 facing up-right, other two mirrored, until the skeleton is ready.
- [ ] S.5 New Gaha parts sheet for the Blender skeleton (asked by Baiyee 8 Nov 22:39 to 22:43, voice notes 1256 and 1257 plus the typed spec: relaxed standing pose like his neutral stance, front three-quarter view facing down-right, same size as his in-game sprite, parts split at the real joints, cloak and skirt as whole pieces, transparent or white background; "if it works for Gaha we'd do the same for everyone and you'd only need to draw the special moves frame by frame"). Suleiman answered 9 Nov 06:27 to 06:37 with four pictures and no text (1265 to 1268, 1086 by 1448 each): 06:27 Gaha full figure standing with the spear (reference), 06:33 body parts split with white gaps (head, necklace, chest, belly, hips, arms, forearms, hands, thighs, shins, feet), 06:35 feather cloak and skirt as two whole pieces on black, 06:37 the spear on black. Not built yet; next step is to test the rig on it (S.3 stays parked until that test). Tested 9 Nov (Blender 5.2 from the command line, saved in Fabien Blueprint/BLENDER TESTS/gaha_rig_v2/, game untouched): Built: 17 body parts cut cleanly on his white gaps, cloak, skirt and spear cut from the black sheets, lined up on the reference with one scale of 0.84 (the sheet draws Gaha 1.19x bigger), 26 bones, soft-deformed cloak, skirt and torso, 12-frame breathing idle and 8-frame walk, rendered front-on. Verdict: idle is good (breathing, late head, cloak and skirt sway keep his drawing intact); the front-on walk reads as a march in place; left arm and spear grip are only approximate (sheet arms hang straight, reference holds the spear) and the cloak is one back piece so it does not wrap the shoulders; faint light line at some joints. Three-quarter views need a parts sheet per facing (down-right and up-right, left mirrored) with the cloak split into front mantle, back drape and side panels, extra overlap drawn at the joints, and weights redone per facing; a body that truly twists needs real 3D. Recommendation unchanged from S.1: rig for idle and small reactions once three-quarter sheets exist, frame-by-frame for walks and attacks. S.3 stays parked. RIG V3 9 Nov (Baiyee, test only, game untouched; Fabien Blueprint/BLENDER TESTS/gaha_rig_v3/, script build_gaha_rig_v3.py, GIF and MP4 for Suleiman, contact sheets): Suleiman's 15:12 to 15:57 sheets (three-quarter figures 1282, 1283, 1284; cloak split into mantle, two side pieces, back drape and kilt 1287 and 1290; body parts in the three-quarter view 1288 and 1291) cut into 17 body parts and 5 cloak pieces. Built: spear Child Of the fist bone (position only, so the shaft stays upright, gripped through the fist); IK so the other hand follows a point on the shaft; IK on both legs with ankle targets fixed in the world so the feet stay planted while the hips sway and dip; hair as damped spring bones baked to keys; cloak, mantle, side pieces and kilt as Blender cloth pinned at the shoulders and belt, colliding with 8 invisible body proxies that follow the bones. Checks: 0 cloth vertices inside a collider in every rendered frame of idle (12) and walk (8); stress test (a block rammed into the mantle) pushed it out from y -0.17 to -0.33 with 0 penetrations over 48 frames. Verdict: the physics side works and the pieces assemble into his drawing; it is good as an offline tool for idle and small reactions, not yet for the walk. Weak: (1) the cloth is flat pictures seen straight on, so draping in depth is invisible and only the sideways swing and settle shows (hems move 9 to 56 mm), the side pieces read as wings; (2) loops are not perfectly periodic: the last frame differs from the first by 1.65 times a normal step in idle and 1.5 times in the walk (cloth hems 2 to 64 mm off between cycles), a small hitch in the GIF; (3) hair is part of the head picture, so it bends as a mass; the motion is subtle and I did not measure its angles; (4) the walk is still a front-on march in place, it needs side and back parts sheets (as Suleiman said); (5) the IK arm turns his relaxed one-hand hold into a two-hand guard, the right forearm crosses the belly, the right shoulder cap shows above the mantle and one clasp is hidden; (6) the spear does not tilt with the hand; (7) thin gap at the neck. Recommendation: unchanged, bake idle and reaction frames from the rig, keep walks and attacks frame by frame; per-facing parts sheets (down-right, up-right) needed before any game use. S.3 stays parked.
